<h2>Coverage and method</h2>
<p class="gm-method">MATIF rapeseed and the CZCE rapeseed, rapeseed oil and rapeseed meal rankings are the two legs.</p>
<p class="gm-method">Sector: Agriculture, Oilseeds. Units print per row and are never converted. Category definitions are each source's own. Pages render the current corrected vintage of each source; first-release vintages are not kept. An extreme reading is a condition of crowding, not a buy or sell signal.</p>
